## Coverage

- all: 1876 identifier(s)

## Design system components

788 component(s) carry a stable `@uxid`. 34 declare variants (40 axis/axes, 110 value(s) in total), read from `cva()` calls, class maps, prop unions and conditional class branches.

19 of them are primitives under `lib/core/design_system`, identified by a `lib/core/design_system` directory of 21 files. 494 are application components, of which 158 are rendered in exactly one other file and 166 are rendered nowhere else (a page, or dead code).

| @uxid | component | file | variants | states | uses | notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| "lib/app.dart::_MissingRouteDataScreen" | _MissingRouteDataScreen | `lib/app.dart` |  |  | 0 | Fallback screen shown when a route is opened without the data it requires. |
| "lib/app.dart::ReserveKeyboardDismissBar" | ReserveKeyboardDismissBar | `lib/app.dart` |  |  | 5 | App-level wrapper that reserves space for, or attaches, the keyboard dismiss bar above the on-screen keyboard. |
